I haven’t really noticed that exactly. It’s more like I’m just… trying to get away from where I am, I guess. I’m not sure about feeling outside myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had periods where you felt unusually energized, needed less sleep, or felt like your thoughts were racing? I’ve had low days, but… not something I’m sure was most of the day every day.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you no longer take any pleasure or interest in activities you used to enjoy, even when you make yourself do them?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess so.
